    51   const location = useLocation();

    56

    58   const [toolboxOpen, setToolboxOpen] = useState(() => isToolboxActive);
    59 
    60   // 进入工具箱路径时保持展开，离开时不强制关闭（保留用户状态）
    61   useEffect(() => {
    62     if (isToolboxActive) setToolboxOpen(true);
    63   }, [isToolboxActive]);
    64 
    65   // 子菜单导航：工具箱保持展开，仅移动端侧边栏关闭
    66   const handleToolNav = (path: string) => {
    67     navigate(path);
    68     onNavigate?.();
    69   };
    70 
    71   // 主按钮点击：激活状态下不允许折叠（防止 useEffect 抖动）
    72   const handleToolboxToggle = () => {
    73     if (isToolboxActive) {
    74       // 已在工具箱页面：只允许展开，不折叠
    75       setToolboxOpen(true);
    76     } else {
    77       setToolboxOpen(v => !v);
    78     }
    79   };

}

export default function VoiceCallModal({ open, onClose }: VoiceCallModalProps) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<CallPhase>('idle');
  const [muted, setMuted] = useState(false);
  const [speakerOn, setSpeakerOn] = useState(true);
  const [camOn, setCamOn] = useState(true);
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  const ringTimeoutRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  // 打开时重置并开始拨号
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setPhase('ringing');
      setElapsed(0);
      setMuted(false);
      setSpeakerOn(true);
      setCamOn(true);
      // 模拟2.5秒后接通
      ringTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setPhase('connected'), 2500);
    } else {
      setPhase('idle');
      clearTimers();
    }
    return () => clearTimers();
  }, [open]);

  // 计时器
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === 'connected') {
      timerRef.current = setInterval(() => setElapsed(e => e + 1), 1000);
    } else {
      if (timerRef.current) { clearInterval(timerRef.current); timerRef.current = null; }
    }
  }, [phase]);

  const clearTimers = () => {
    if (timerRef.current) { clearInterval(timerRef.current); timerRef.current = null; }
    if (ringTimeoutRef.current) { clearTimeout(ringTimeoutRef.current); ringTimeoutRef.current = null; }
  };

  const handleHangup = () => {
    clearTimers();
    onClose();
  };

  const formatTime = (s: number) => `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center"
        >
          {/* 背景蒙层 */}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={handleHangup} />

          <motion.div
            initial={{ scale: 0.92, opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ scale: 0.92, opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
            transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 300, damping: 28 }}
            className="relative w-[340px] md:w-[380px] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"
            style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #1a2035 0%, #0d1220 100%)' }}
            onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            {/* ── 拨号中界面 ── */}
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
              {phase === 'ringing' && (
                <motion.div
                  key="ringing"
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  className="flex flex-col items-center px-8 py-12 gap-6"
                >
                  {/* 脉冲头像 */}
                  <div className="relative">
                    {[1, 2, 3].map(i => (
                      <motion.div
                        key={i}
                        className="absolute inset-0 rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/30"
                        animate={{ scale: [1, 1.18 + i * 0.1], opacity: [0.6, 0] }}
                        transition={{ duration: 1.8, repeat: Infinity, delay: i * 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }}
                      />
                    ))}
                    <img
                      src={TEACHER_AVATAR}
                      alt="老师头像"
                      className="w-28 h-28 rounded-2xl object-cover relative z-10 ring-2 ring-sky-400/40"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <p className="text-white text-2xl font-semibold tracking-wide">{TEACHER_NUM}</p>
                    <p className="text-sky-300/70 text-sm mt-1">{TEACHER_NAME}</p>
                    <motion.p
                      className="text-white/50 text-xs mt-2"
                      animate={{ opacity: [0.4, 1, 0.4] }}
                      transition={{ duration: 1.4, repeat: Infinity }}
                    >
                      正在呼叫...
                    </motion.p>
                  </div>
                  {/* 挂断按钮 */}
                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleHangup}
                      className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 active:scale-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-red-500/30"
                    >
                      <PhoneOff className="w-7 h-7 text-white" />
                    </button>
                    <span className="text-white/50 text-xs mt-1">挂断</span>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}

              {/* ── 通话中界面 ── */}
              {phase === 'connected' && (
                <motion.div
                  key="connected"
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  className="relative"
                >
                  {/* 对方全屏画面（老师背景） */}
                  <div className="relative w-full h-[480px] md:h-[520px] overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={TEACHER_AVATAR}
                      alt="老师画面"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top"
                    />
                    {/* 暗色遮罩 */}
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/10 via-transparent to-black/60" />

                    {/* 右上角：自己小画面 */}
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                      transition={{ delay: 0.3 }}
                      className="absolute top-4 right-4 w-20 h-24 rounded-xl overflow-hidden ring-2 ring-white/40 shadow-lg bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      {camOn ? (
                        <div className="w-full h-full bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-600 to-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center">
                          <Video className="w-6 h-6 text-white/40" />
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-full h-full bg-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center">
                          <VideoOff className="w-5 h-5 text-white/30" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </motion.div>

                    {/* 通话时长 */}
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2">
                      <span className="text-white/80 text-sm font-mono bg-black/30 rounded-full px-3 py-1 backdrop-blur-sm">
                        {formatTime(elapsed)}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* 底部控制栏 */}
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 px-6 pb-7 pt-8">
                      <div className="flex items-end justify-around">
                        {/* 静音 */}
                        <CtrlBtn
                          icon={muted ? MicOff : Mic}
                          label={muted ? '取消静音' : '静音'}
                          active={muted}
                          onClick={() => setMuted(v => !v)}
                        />
                        {/* 扬声器 */}
                        <CtrlBtn
                          icon={speakerOn ? Volume2 : VolumeX}
                          label={speakerOn ? '关扬声器' : '开扬声器'}
                          active={!speakerOn}
                          onClick={() => setSpeakerOn(v => !v)}
                        />
                        {/* 挂断 — 红色中央大按钮 */}
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={handleHangup}
                            className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 active:scale-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl shadow-red-500/40"
                          >
                            <PhoneOff className="w-7 h-7 text-white" />
                          </button>
                          <span className="text-white/60 text-xs">挂断</span>
                        </div>
                        {/* 摄像头 */}
                        <CtrlBtn
                          icon={camOn ? Video : VideoOff}
                          label={camOn ? '关摄像头' : '开摄像头'}
                          active={!camOn}
                          onClick={() => setCamOn(v => !v)}
                        />
                        {/* 切换语音 */}
                        <CtrlBtn
                          icon={Phone}
                          label="切换语音"
                          active={false}
                          onClick={() => {}}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

// ── 控制按钮子组件 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
function CtrlBtn({ icon: Icon, label, active, onClick }: {
  icon: React.ElementType;
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClick}
        className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all active:scale-90 ${
          active ? 'bg-white/20 ring-1 ring-white/30' : 'bg-black/40 hover:bg-black/60'
        }`}
      >
        <Icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${active ? 'text-white' : 'text-white/80'}`} />
      </button>
      <span className="text-white/50 text-[10px] whitespace-nowrap">{label}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

    82 
    83   useEffect(() => { loadPosts(); }, [filterType, sortBy, activeCircle]);
    84 
    85   const loadPosts = async () => {
    86     setLoading(true);
    87     let query = supabase.from('community_posts').select('*');
    88     if (filterType !== 'all') query = query.eq('post_type', filterType);
    89     if (sortBy === 'hot') query = query.order('likes_count', { ascending: false });
    90     else query = query.order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    91     const { data } = await query.limit(30);
    92     setPosts(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    93     setLoading(false);
    94   };
    95 
    96   const loadReplies = async (postId: string) => {
    97     if (repliesMap[postId]) return;
    98     setReplyLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: true }));
    99     const { data } = await supabase.from('community_replies').select('*').eq('post_id', postId).order('created_at', { ascending: true }).limit(50);
   100     setRepliesMap(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: Array.isArray(data) ? data : [] }));

    26   { icon: BookOpen, text: '输入任何学习问题，AI 将实时流式回答' },
    27   { icon: Sparkles, text: '支持导出对话为 Markdown 文件' },
    28 ];
    29 
    30 // 苏格拉底式辅导系统提示词
    31 const SOCRATIC_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `你是一位采用苏格拉底教学法的AI导师。你的核心原则：
    32 1. 【不直接给答案】遇到问题先用提问引导学生思考，例如"你觉得这个概念的核心是什么？"
    33 2. 【分步引导】将复杂问题拆分，每次只引导一个思考方向
    34 3. 【反问促思】当学生回答后，追问"为什么这样认为？""有没有例外情况？"
    35 4. 【肯定鼓励】对学生的思考给予正向反馈，增强学习信心
    36 5. 【适时总结】在学生经过思考得出答案后，帮助归纳和强化知识点
    37 请始终以引导者而非答题者的角色出现，让学生通过自己的思考获得顿悟。`;
    38 
    39 // 直接回答系统提示词
    40 const DIRECT_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `你是智学伴AI答疑助手，为高校学生提供精准、清晰的学习辅导。
    41 回答时请：简洁明了、逻辑清晰、配合示例、适当使用Markdown格式增强可读性。`;
    42 
    43 export default function TutoringPage() {
    44   const { user } = useAuth();
    45   const [initialMessages, setInitialMessages] = useState<ChatMsg[]>([]);
    46   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    47   const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMsg[]>([]);
    48   const [socraticMode, setSocraticMode] = useState(false);
    49   const [teacherCollapsed, setTeacherCollapsed] = useState(false);
    50   const [lastAIMsg, setLastAIMsg] = useState('');
    51   const [voiceCallOpen, setVoiceCallOpen] = useState(false);
    52 
    53   useEffect(() => {
    54     if (!user) { setLoading(false); return; }
    55     supabase.from('chat_messages')
    56       .select('*')
    57       .eq('user_id', user.id)
    58       .eq('session_type', 'tutoring')
    59       .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    60       .limit(50)
    61       .then(({ data }) => {
    62         const welcomeMsg = socraticMode
    63           ? '你好！我是智学伴的**苏格拉底式AI导师** 🤔\n\n我不会直接给出答案，而是通过提问引导你自己找到答案。这样能帮助你真正理解知识，而不只是记住答案。\n\n遇到任何问题，和我一起思考吧！'
    64           : '你好！我是智学伴智能答疑助手。支持文字和图片提问。请问有什么可以帮助你？';
    65         const msgs = Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0
    66           ? data.map(m => ({ id: m.id, role: m.role as 'user' | 'assistant', content: m.content }))
    67           : [{ id: 'welcome', role: 'assistant' as const, content: welcomeMsg }];
    68         setInitialMessages(msgs);
    69         setMessages(msgs);
    70         setLoading(false);
    71       });
    72   }, [user]);
    73 
    74   const handleSaveMessage = async (msg: ChatMsg) => {
    75     if (!user) return;
    76     setMessages(prev => {
    77       const exists = prev.find(m => m.id === msg.id);
    78       return exists ? prev.map(m => m.id === msg.id ? msg : m) : [...prev, msg];
    79     });
    80     // 记录最新 AI 消息，驱动数字人表情/说话
    81     if (msg.role === 'assistant') setLastAIMsg(msg.content);
    82     try {
    83       await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
    84         user_id: user.id, session_type: 'tutoring', role: msg.role, content: msg.content,
    85       });
    86     } catch (err) { console.error('保存消息失败:', err); }
    87   };
    88 
    89 
    90   const exportMarkdown = () => {
    91     if (messages.length === 0) { toast.warning('暂无对话内容'); return; }
    92     const md = messages.map(m => {
    93       const role = m.role === 'user' ? '**我**' : '**智学伴 AI**';
    94       return `${role}\n\n${m.content}`;
    95     }).join('\n\n---\n\n');
    96     const blob = new Blob([`# 智学伴 答疑对话记录\n\n${md}`], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
    97     const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    98     const a = document.createElement('a');
    99     a.href = url; a.download = `答疑记录-${new Date().toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')}.md`;
   100     a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   101     toast.success('对话已导出为 Markdown 文件');
   102   };
   103 
   104   return (
   105     <AppLayout>
   106       <div className="space-y-4">
   107         <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
   108           <h1 className="text-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
   109             <MessageCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
   110             智能答疑
   111             {/* 语音通话按钮 — 紧靠"智能答疑"文字右边 */}
   112             <Button
   113               size="sm"
   114               onClick={() => setVoiceCallOpen(true)}
   115               className="gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-500 to-violet-500 hover:from-sky-600 hover:to-violet-600 text-white border-0 shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20 ml-1"
   116             >
   117               <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   118               语音通话
   119             </Button>
   120           </h1>
   121           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
   122             {/* 苏格拉底模式切换 */}
   123             <button
   124               type="button"
   125               onClick={() => {
   126                 setSocraticMode(!socraticMode);
   127                 toast.success(!socraticMode ? '已开启苏格拉底式引导模式 🤔' : '已切换回直接回答模式 ⚡');
   128               }}
   129               className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition-all ${
   130                 socraticMode
   131                   ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/20 dark:border-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300'
   132                   : 'border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-amber-300 hover:text-amber-600'
   133               }`}
   134             >
   135               {socraticMode ? <Brain className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
   136               {socraticMode ? '苏格拉底引导模式' : '直接回答模式'}
   137             </button>
   138 
   139             <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5" onClick={exportMarkdown}>
   140               <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   141               导出对话
   142             </Button>
   143           </div>
   144         </div>
   145 
   146         {/* 多模态解答形式选择器已移除 */}
   147 
   148         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
   149           {/* 左侧：数字人老师面板 */}
   150           <div className={`lg:col-span-1 ${teacherCollapsed ? 'lg:col-span-1' : ''}`}>
   151             <Card className="h-[calc(100vh-280px)] min-h-[480px] overflow-hidden">
   152               <CardContent className="p-3 h-full">
   153                 <DigitalTeacher
   154                   lastAIMessage={lastAIMsg}
   155                   loading={loading}
   156                   collapsed={teacherCollapsed}
   157                   onToggle={() => setTeacherCollapsed(v => !v)}
   158                 />
   159               </CardContent>
   160             </Card>
   161           </div>
   162 
   163           {/* 中间：对话区域 */}
   164           <div className={teacherCollapsed ? 'lg:col-span-3' : 'lg:col-span-2'}>
   165             <Card className="h-[calc(100vh-280px)] min-h-[480px] flex flex-col">
   166               <CardHeader className="pb-3 shrink-0">
   167                 <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
   168                   {socraticMode
   169                     ? <Brain className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
   170                     : <Bot className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
   171                   }
   172                   {socraticMode ? '苏格拉底式引导助手' : '智能答疑助手'}
   173                   {socraticMode && (
   174                     <span className="ml-1 text-[10px] bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-normal">
   175                       引导思考模式
   176                     </span>
   177                   )}
   178                   <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-muted-foreground font-normal">支持多模态输入</span>
   179                 </CardTitle>
   180               </CardHeader>
   181               <CardContent className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0">
   182                 {loading ? (
   183                   <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full">
   184                     <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
   185                       <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
   186                       <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">加载历史消息...</p>
   187                     </div>
   188                   </div>
   189                 ) : (
   190                   <AIChatPanel
   191                     sessionType="tutoring"
   192                     initialMessages={initialMessages}
   193                     onMessageSent={handleSaveMessage}
   194                     onReplyReceived={handleSaveMessage}
   195                     suggestedQuestions={suggestedQuestions}
   196                     placeholder={socraticMode ? '提出你的问题，AI 将引导你自己思考...' : '输入你的学习问题，AI将实时流式回答...'}
   197                     systemPrompt={socraticMode ? SOCRATIC_SYSTEM_PROMPT : DIRECT_SYSTEM_PROMPT}

   206             {/* 苏格拉底模式说明 */}
   207             {socraticMode && (
   208               <Card className="border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 bg-amber-50/50 dark:bg-amber-900/10">
   209                 <CardHeader className="pb-2">
   210                   <CardTitle className="text-sm flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">
   211                     <Brain className="w-4 h-4" />苏格拉底教学法
   212                   </CardTitle>
   213                 </CardHeader>
   214                 <CardContent className="space-y-2">
   215                   {[

   217                     '💭 通过提问发现问题本质',

   393   // ── 拖拽重排（兼容旧列表模式，画板模式用鼠标拖拽）──
   394   const handleDrop = async (dropIndex: number) => {
   395     if (dragIndex === null || dragIndex === dropIndex || !path) return;
   396     const stages = [...path.stages];
   397     const [moved] = stages.splice(dragIndex, 1);
   398     stages.splice(dropIndex, 0, moved);
   399     const reordered = stages.map((s, i) => ({ ...s, order: i + 1 }));
   400     const currentStage = reordered.findIndex(s => !s.completed);
   401     const { data } = await supabase.from('learning_paths').update({
   402       stages: reordered,
   403       current_stage: currentStage >= 0 ? currentStage : reordered.length - 1,
   404     }).eq('id', path.id).select().maybeSingle();
   405     if (data) { setPath(data); toast.success('顺序已更新'); }
   406     setDragIndex(null);
   407   };
   408 
   409   // ── 画板鼠标拖拽（仅 select 工具时生效）──
   410   const handleNodeMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent, stageId: string) => {
   411     if (activeTool === 'delete') { deleteStage(stageId); return; }
   412     if (activeTool !== 'select') return;
   413     e.preventDefault();
   414     const pos = nodePositions[stageId] ?? { x: 0, y: 0 };
   415     dragStartRef.current = { mx: e.clientX, my: e.clientY, ox: pos.x, oy: pos.y };
   416     setDraggingId(stageId);
   417   }, [activeTool, nodePositions]);
   418 
   419   useEffect(() => {
   420     if (!draggingId) return;
   421     const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
   422       if (!dragStartRef.current) return;
   423       const dx = e.clientX - dragStartRef.current.mx;
   424       const dy = e.clientY - dragStartRef.current.my;
   425       setNodePositions(prev => ({
   426         ...prev,
   427         [draggingId]: { x: Math.max(0, dragStartRef.current!.ox + dx), y: Math.max(0, dragStartRef.current!.oy + dy) },
   428       }));
   429     };
   430     const onUp = () => { setDraggingId(null); dragStartRef.current = null; };
   431     window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
   432     window.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
   433     return () => { window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove); window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp); };
   434   }, [draggingId]);
   435 
   436   const completedCount = path?.stages.filter(s => s.completed).length ?? 0;
   437 
   438   if (loading) return (
   439     <AppLayout>
   440       <div className="space-y-4">
   441         <Skeleton className="h-24 bg-muted rounded-2xl" />
   442         <Skeleton className="h-80 bg-muted rounded-2xl" />
   443       </div>
   444     </AppLayout>
   445   );
   446 
   447   return (
   448     <AppLayout>
   449       <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 h-full">
   450 
   451         {/* ══ 资源类型选择器 ══ */}
   452         <div className="shrink-0">
   453           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 gap-2">
   454             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   455               <Route className="w-5 h-5 text-primary shrink-0" />
   456               <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">学习路径</h1>
   457               {path && (
   458                 <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
   459                   <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1">{completedCount}/{path.stages.length} 阶段</Badge>
   460                   <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary border-0">{path.progress_percent}% 完成</Badge>
   461                 </div>
   462               )}
   463             </div>
   464             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
   465               {/* 缩放 */}
   466               {path && (
   467                 <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-muted rounded-lg p-1">
   468                   <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoom(z => Math.max(0.6, +(z - 0.1).toFixed(1)))}
   469                     className="w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center rounded hover:bg-background transition-colors text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
   470                     <ZoomOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   471                   </button>
   472                   <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground w-10 text-center">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
   473                   <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoom(z => Math.min(1.4, +(z + 0.1).toFixed(1)))}
   474                     className="w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center rounded hover:bg-background transition-colors text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
   475                     <ZoomIn className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   476                   </button>

   479                     <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
   480                   </button>
   481                 </div>
   482               )}
   483               {path && (

   485                   className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-border hover:bg-muted transition-colors text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
   486                   onClick={async () => {

    29 function tagColor(tag: string) {
    30   let h = 0;
    31   for (let i = 0; i < tag.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + tag.charCodeAt(i)) % TAG_COLORS.length;
    32   return TAG_COLORS[h];

    35 function formatDate(s: string) {
    36   const d = new Date(s);
    37   const now = new Date();
    38   const diff = (now.getTime() - d.getTime()) / 1000;
    39   if (diff < 60) return '刚刚';
    40   if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60)}分钟前`;
    41   if (diff < 86400) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3600)}小时前`;
    42   return d.toLocaleDateString('zh-CN', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    43 }

    46 function insertMarkdown(textarea: HTMLTextAreaElement | null, before: string, after = '', placeholder = '') {
    47   if (!textarea) return '';
    48   const { selectionStart: s, selectionEnd: e, value: v } = textarea;
    49   const selected = v.slice(s, e) || placeholder;
    50   const newVal = v.slice(0, s) + before + selected + after + v.slice(e);
    51   return newVal;
    52 }

    13 /* ─── Markdown 渲染器（无需第三方库） ─── */
    14 function MarkdownContent({ content, isUser }: { content: string; isUser: boolean }) {
    15   if (isUser) {
    16     return <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-pretty">{content}</span>;
    17   }
    18   const lines = content.split('\n');
    19   const elements: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    20   let i = 0;
    21   while (i < lines.length) {
    22     const line = lines[i];
    23     // H1 / H2 / H3
    24     if (/^### /.test(line)) {
    25       elements.push(<h3 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^### /, ''))}</h3>);
    26     } else if (/^## /.test(line)) {
    27       elements.push(<h2 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1.5 text-foreground border-b border-border/40 pb-1">{renderInline(line.replace(/^## /, ''))}</h2>);
    28     } else if (/^# /.test(line)) {
    29       elements.push(<h1 key={i} className="text-base font-extrabold mt-3 mb-2 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^# /, ''))}</h1>);
    30     // 有序列表
    31     } else if (/^\d+\. /.test(line.trimStart())) {
    32       const indent = line.match(/^(\s*)/)?.[1]?.length ?? 0;
    33       elements.push(
    34         <div key={i} className={`flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed ${indent > 0 ? 'ml-4' : ''}`}>
    35           <span className="text-primary font-semibold shrink-0 min-w-[1.2rem]">{line.trimStart().match(/^(\d+)\./)?.[1]}.</span>
    36           <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^\d+\. /, ''))}</span>
    37         </div>
    38       );
    39     // 无序列表 - / * / •
    40     } else if (/^[-*•] /.test(line.trimStart())) {
    41       const indent = line.match(/^(\s*)/)?.[1]?.length ?? 0;
    42       elements.push(
    43         <div key={i} className={`flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed ${indent > 0 ? 'ml-4' : ''}`}>
    44           <span className="text-primary shrink-0 mt-1">•</span>
    45           <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^[-*•] /, ''))}</span>
    46         </div>
    47       );
    48     // 代码块开始
    49     } else if (/^```/.test(line)) {
    50       const lang = line.replace(/^```/, '').trim();
    51       const codeLines: string[] = [];
    52       i++;
    53       while (i < lines.length && !lines[i].startsWith('```')) {
    54         codeLines.push(lines[i]);
    55         i++;
    56       }
    57       elements.push(
    58         <div key={i} className="my-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/60">
    59           {lang && <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-muted/80 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground font-mono border-b border-border/40">{lang}</div>}
    60           <pre className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed overflow-x-auto bg-muted/50 text-foreground">
    61             <code>{codeLines.join('\n')}</code>
    62           </pre>
    63         </div>
    64       );
    65     // 分割线
    66     } else if (/^---+$/.test(line.trim())) {
    67       elements.push(<hr key={i} className="my-3 border-border/40" />);
    68     // 引用块
    69     } else if (/^> /.test(line)) {
    70       elements.push(
    71         <blockquote key={i} className="border-l-2 border-primary/40 pl-3 my-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground italic">
    72           {renderInline(line.replace(/^> /, ''))}
    73         </blockquote>
    74       );
    75     // 空行
    76     } else if (line.trim() === '') {
    77       if (elements.length > 0) elements.push(<div key={i} className="h-1.5" />);
    78     // 普通段落
    79     } else {
    80       elements.push(<p key={i} className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-pretty">{renderInline(line)}</p>);
    81     }
    82     i++;
    83   }
    84   return <div className="space-y-0.5">{elements}</div>;
    85 }
    86 
    87 /* 内联样式：**粗体** `代码` */
    88 function renderInline(text: string): React.ReactNode {
    89   const parts: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    90   const regex = /(\*\*(.+?)\*\*|`([^`]+)`|\*([^*]+)\*)/g;
    91   let last = 0;
    92   let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    93   while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    94     if (match.index > last) parts.push(text.slice(last, match.index));
    95     if (match[2]) parts.push(<strong key={match.index} className="font-semibold text-foreground">{match[2]}</strong>);
    96     else if (match[3]) parts.push(<code key={match.index} className="px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-muted/70 text-xs font-mono text-primary">{match[3]}</code>);
    97     else if (match[4]) parts.push(<em key={match.index} className="italic">{match[4]}</em>);
    98     last = match.index + match[0].length;
    99   }
   100   if (last < text.length) parts.push(text.slice(last));
   101   return parts.length === 1 ? parts[0] : parts;
   102 }

   571                     </div>

   110   const handleLike = async (post: Post) => {
   111     if (!user) { toast.error('请先登录'); return; }
   112     const isLiked = post.is_liked;
   113     const newLikes = isLiked ? Math.max(0, post.likes_count - 1) : post.likes_count + 1;
   114     setPosts(prev => prev.map(p => p.id === post.id ? { ...p, likes_count: newLikes, is_liked: !isLiked } : p));
   115     await supabase.from('community_posts').update({ likes_count: newLikes }).eq('id', post.id);
   116   };
   117 
   118   const handleCreatePost = async () => {
   119     if (!user) { toast.error('请先登录'); return; }
   120     if (!newPost.title.trim() || !newPost.content.trim()) { toast.error('标题和内容不能为空'); return; }
   121     const { error } = await supabase.from('community_posts').insert({
   122       user_id: user.id, title: newPost.title, content: newPost.content, post_type: newPost.type, likes_count: 0, replies_count: 0,
   123     });
   124     if (error) { toast.error('发布失败：' + error.message); return; }
   125     toast.success('发布成功！');

   135     setReplyLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: true }));
   136     const { error } = await supabase.from('community_replies').insert({ post_id: postId, user_id: user.id, content: text });
   137     if (error) { toast.error('回复失败'); setReplyLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: false })); return; }
   138     const post = posts.find(p => p.id === postId);
   139     if (post) await supabase.from('community_posts').update({ replies_count: (post.replies_count ?? 0) + 1 }).eq('id', postId);
   140     setReplyText(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: '' }));
   141     setPosts(prev => prev.map(p => p.id === postId ? { ...p, replies_count: (p.replies_count ?? 0) + 1 } : p));
   142     toast.success('回复成功！');
   143     const { data } = await supabase.from('community_replies').select('*').eq('post_id', postId).order('created_at', { ascending: true });

   161   const formatTime = (d: string) => {
   162     const diff = Date.now() - new Date(d).getTime();
   163     const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
   164     if (mins < 1) return '刚刚';
   165     if (mins < 60) return `${mins}分钟前`;
   166     const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
   167     if (hours < 24) return `${hours}小时前`;
   168     return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)}天前`;
   169   };
   170

   722                         <line x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2}
   723                           stroke={stage.completed ? 'hsl(var(--primary))' : 'hsl(var(--border))'}
   724                           strokeWidth="2.5" strokeDasharray={stage.completed ? '0' : '7 5'}
   725                           strokeLinecap="round"
   726                           markerEnd={stage.completed ? 'url(#arrowDone)' : 'url(#arrowTodo)'} />
   727                         {stage.completed && (
   728                           <circle r="4" fill="hsl(var(--primary))" opacity="0.9">
   729                             <animateMotion dur="1.6s" repeatCount="indefinite"
   730                               path={`M${x1},${y1} L${x2},${y2}`} />
   731                           </circle>
   732                         )}
   733                       </g>
   734                     );
   735                   })}
   736                 </svg>
   737 
   738                 {/* 绝对定位节点 */}
   739                 {path.stages.map((stage, index) => {
   740                   const isCurrent = index === path.current_stage && !stage.completed;
   741                   const pos = nodePositions[stage.id] ?? { x: 48 + index * 290, y: 40 };
   742                   return (
   743                     <div
   744                       key={stage.id}
   745                       style={{ position: 'absolute', left: pos.x, top: pos.y, width: 260 }}
   746                       onMouseDown={e => handleNodeMouseDown(e, stage.id)}
   747                     >
   748                       <StageNode
   749                         stage={stage}
   750                         index={index}
   751                         isCurrent={isCurrent}
   752                         isCompleted={stage.completed}
   753                         isLocked={!stage.completed && !isCurrent}
   754                         onComplete={() => completeStage(stage.id)}
   755                         completing={completingId === stage.id}
   756                         onDelete={() => deleteStage(stage.id)}
   757                         onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; setDragIndex(index); }}
   758                         onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move'; }}
   759                         onDrop={e => { e.preventDefault(); handleDrop(index); }}
   760                         isDragging={draggingId === stage.id}
   761                         activeTool={activeTool}
   762                       />
   763                     </div>
   764                   );
   765                 })}
   766 
   767                 {/* 新增节点表单（浮动在画布左上空白处） */}
   768                 <AnimatePresence>
   769                   {addingNode && (
   770                     <motion.div
   771                       initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.92 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
   772                       exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.92 }}
   773                       className="absolute z-30 bg-card rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-primary/50 p-4 space-y-3 shadow-xl"
   774                       style={{ left: 48, top: 320, width: 260 }}
   775                     >
   776                       <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5">
   777                         <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />新增学习节点
   778                       </p>
   779                       <input
   780                         ref={addInputRef}
   781                         value={newNodeTitle}
   782                         onChange={e => setNewNodeTitle(e.target.value)}
   783                         onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') addStage(); if (e.key === 'Escape') { setAddingNode(false); setNewNodeTitle(''); setNewNodeDesc(''); } }}
   784                         placeholder="节点名称（必填）"
   785                         className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:border-primary placeholder:text-muted-foreground/50"
   786                       />
   787                       <textarea
   788                         value={newNodeDesc}
   789                         onChange={e => setNewNodeDesc(e.target.value)}
   790                         placeholder="描述（可选）"
   791                         rows={2}
   792                         className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:border-primary resize-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/50"
   793                       />
   794                       <div className="flex gap-2">
   795                         <Button size="sm" className="flex-1 h-7 text-xs" onClick={addStage} disabled={!newNodeTitle.trim()}>确认</Button>
   796                         <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="h-7 text-xs px-2" onClick={() => { setAddingNode(false); setNewNodeTitle(''); setNewNodeDesc(''); }}>
   797                           <X className="w-3 h-3" />
   798                         </Button>
   799                       </div>
   800                     </motion.div>

   536         </Tabs>
   537       </div>
   538     </AppLayout>
   539   );
   540 }
